- **Three building languages** in the reference's scale, view and even light: shell houses (the hearths'), cast houses (the Council's), carved stone (the Temple's), in a stepped quarry bowl; ~70–100 big buildings, not ~450 small ones; every place nameable at a glance.
- **The land:** amber sun, black nights, cap-trees, lichen, glow-moss, pit beasts, skitters, lamp-moths; the city up the road; the war as a red glow past the mountains and as pressure at home (quota, levy, wounded, refugees).
- **Every crystal is violet at its heart**; affinity is a second colour inside it.
- §16.1 is absorbed into this (its "carapace / hide / cast" direction stands; its details are superseded by `docs/theme.md` §5).
A **THEME** builder makes the shared kit (`src/render/theme/`: palette constants, the wickfolk model, building-kit primitives, the model sheets) that the people, town and interface builders draw from.

## 18. The town's visual target (owner, 2026-11-23 — binding; replaces `town.png` as the town's target and overrides §16.1 / §17 where they differ)
The owner generated `docs/refs/town-target.png` and said: **"The mushrooms are too small here and it's too bright but otherwise not bad."** It is the town's binding target with those two corrections. (`town.png` stays as a secondary reference for the hut forms; `mine.png` stays the lighting and line reference for the whole game.)

**Take from the image, as drawn:**
- **The view:** three-quarter top-down; every building shows its roof and its front wall; nothing shrinks with distance; no horizon, no sky.
- **The ground:** the town lies on the floor of an open quarry. Stepped teal-gray rock **terraces** ring it and push into it as cliff blocks with drawn faces (lit top, darker face, cracks); stairs are cut into the rock; **cave dwellings** with gold light are cut into the terrace faces.
- **The lanes:** a web of ochre dirt lanes that closes into rounded blocks of 4–8 buildings, all meeting at a **round central plaza** with a crystal monument ringed by market awnings.
- **The Temple:** carved into the tall cliff at the top of the town — a dark gate with gold figures and inlay, gold obelisk-lamps, broad steps down to the plaza.
- **Shell houses:** clusters of ribbed teal vaults and rust / red hide roofs, lumpy, each a little different, clutter round them.
- **Cast houses:** cream cylinders and blocks with dark pipes, domed tanks, chimneys; round vats of teal liquid; one large cast compound on its own terrace (the Keep and works); black lattice headframes / derricks.
- **Crystal:** fenced **sorting pens** heaped with violet crystals; **open crystal pits** with rails, trestles, cranes and violet crystals in their stepped walls; rail trestles between terraces.
- **Water and growth:** a teal spring pool with flat stones; fungus groves on the terraces.
- **People:** hundreds of tiny gray big-headed figures on every lane.
- **Density and scale:** about 100–130 buildings, each about 3–6 figures wide (this replaces "70–100 buildings, 5–8 figures wide").

**The owner's two corrections:**
1. **Cap-trees are big.** A cap as wide as a house or wider on a thick pale stalk; groves that overhang lanes and roofs; a few giants as landmarks (one by the spring, one by the Temple steps). Small mushrooms only as undergrowth.
2. **Darker.** The image's median luminance is ~108 / 255; `town.png` is 42. By day aim at about 45–60 with every form still readable (muted, low-key, the mine's mood); dusk copper and darker; night darker still with forms faintly readable and lights as accents — never "dots of light on black", never a bright cartoon.

**Owner (2026-11-23, later): "make shrooms big but occluded from view above the trunk until you zoom out more, fade in effect."** Binding:
- A cap-tree is drawn in two parts: the **stalk** (trunk, base, roots, small undergrowth — always drawn, sorted with the people and buildings) and the **cap** (a separate canopy layer above people and roofs).
- **Zoomed in, the cap is not drawn** — you see the stalk rising out of frame and everything under the tree (people, lanes, doors) stays visible; only a soft cap-shaped shade on the ground tells you it is overhead.
- **Zooming out, the cap fades in** smoothly (no pop) until at town zoom it is fully opaque and the groves read as the big canopies of the place.
- One shared rule for every piece: `capAlpha(figurePx)` in the THEME kit (`src/render/theme/props.ts`) — 0 when a figure is ≥ ~40 px tall on screen, 1 when ≤ ~16 px, eased between; the ground shade is `1 − capAlpha` strong. Every renderer that draws cap-trees (town engine, TOWN-ART, world composition, AMBIENT, FINALE) uses it and draws caps on a top layer; wind sways caps only.

**Owner (2026-11-23, later still): "Mine shot's mood, 2's detailing for a town in a sense."** Binding — this settles the two references' roles:
- **Mood and light come from `mine.png`**: low-key and moody; forms revealed by broad pools of local light (cold white shard-lamps, gold hearths and the Temple, violet crystal glow) with soft falloff; deep teal-black shadow between; saturated colour inside the light; the picture falling away to black at the edge of what is known. Measured on `mine.png`'s lit pixels: median luminance 53 / 255, quartiles 37–71, highlights to ~115. The town's known ground should sit in the same range (the target image is 108 median — twice too bright).
- **Detail comes from `town-target.png`**: the density of buildings, lanes, terraces, clutter, pens, pits, figures — every lit square of the town as busy and specific as that image.
- Together: the town looks like the mine reference would if its camera rose out of the shaft — not a bright cartoon map, and not dots of light on black: wide overlapping pools of light in which fully detailed forms read, shadow where no light reaches but with enough faint ambient that roofs and terraces still hold their shapes. By day the quarry is dim amber-gray (the walls shade it) and the pools are weaker; at dusk and night the pools carry the picture.

**[Design] How the image maps onto the game:**
- The **upper-left open pit with the headframes is the pit head**: the lift shaft drops from its floor into the mine the player starts in. The world composition's descent goes through it.
- The **right-hand pit** is the Council's old surface diggings (worked by day, guarded) — a place for events and for the count's carts.
- The **lower-right cast compound** is the Keep and vault; the **vats** are the wash-place / dye and fuel works; the **central plaza** is the market; the lower-centre pens are the sorting pens beside the tally house.
- The wickfolk keep the wick (at this zoom it is a dot of light on each head: gold, pale or violet).
- Fog rule unchanged: Active = colour, Sensed = gray, Void = exact `#000`, no band.

## 19. Order of work: something in place first (owner, 2026-11-23 — binding until the town's look is set)
Owner: **"You also don't have to work too hard on the visuals until that agent is done. Just get something in place for now."** (Orchestrator's reading: "that agent" is the town builder that is redoing the town in the mine reference's mood with the target's detailing, §18.)
- Until that look is established and seen by the owner, **visual pieces stop polishing**. Their goal is: in place, correct in kind and colour (theme, §18), wired into the integrated build, zero console errors — then report. No rounds of side-by-side refinement, no "do not report below 8" for looks in this phase.
- **Priority is the playable loop end to end**: cutscene → bonus → nights and Days in the mine → watershed → the town (Shade) → the ending beat, on the real rules, with the lived-in Day. Rules, play scene, the Day's staging, interface function, the reel.
- The town engine keeps full effort on the look (it is the piece the others wait for). When it lands, visual polish resumes against it.

## 20. Owner's verdict on the shared kit (2026-11-23)
Owner, on `screenshots/theme-props.png`: **"Characters are charming. Props are. NOT EVEN CLOSE."**
- **The wickfolk figure is approved as drawn** (`src/render/theme/folk.ts`, `body.ts`, `kit.ts`): proportions, head, eyes, fins, the wick. Nobody redesigns it; the animator and CAST build on it. (The wick, the boldest [Design] item in `docs/theme.md`, stands.)
- **The props are rejected.** Orchestrator's reading of why, from tight crops of `mine.png` and `town-target.png`: ours are side elevations where the references show everything from the three-quarter top-down camera (top face visible, squat, grounded); ours are clean vector shapes with uniform thin outlines and smooth gradients where the references have a thick, uneven, hand-wobbled dark outline, chunky irregular forms, hatch / crack / chip marks, hard-edged two-or-three-tone shading; ours are toy-bright where the references are muted and low-key with colour only inside pools of light.
- A dedicated **PROPS** builder redraws every shared prop in the references' hand behind the same function names (other pieces keep calling them); its bar is the look itself (the one exception to §19). The rule it sets — view, line, shading, value — then applies to buildings and terrain when visual polish resumes.

## Piece decisions (builders append below)

### P0 — Scaffold
- Ports: dev server 5173; e2e default 5199 (override with `PORT=...`); each piece's harness/e2e uses its own assigned port. `startServer` throws if the port is already in use (it never reuses a foreign server) and kills the vite process group on exit/SIGINT/SIGTERM or `stop()`.

### P1 — Sim core
Engine: `src/sim/**` (pure, seeded mulberry32 PRNG stored in `state.rng`; every action copy-on-writes the state via `structuredClone`, inputs are never mutated; GameState is plain JSON). All tunables live in `src/sim/balance.ts` (`BALANCE`); spec formulas in `src/sim/formulas.ts` are exact and unit-tested. Run `npx tsx scripts/sim-smoke.ts [seeds] [maxTurns]` (or `TRACE=<seed> ... 0`) for pacing numbers.

**Overrides of the draft numbers above**
- XP thresholds: **L2 90, L3 220, L4 460** (L5 700, L6 1400, L7 2600 unchanged). Draft 60/150/300 gave a 9-turn Whisper.
- Evil-act XP = Malice vented **/4** (draft /10; /10 made evil worthless as XP). Miracle XP = crystal cost, eating +1, new follower +5, Devotion marks 25/50/75/100 → +2/+4/+8/+16, zone first made Active +10, desecration +25 (as drafted).
- Hunger: each crystal eaten **−10** (draft −12). Rise 5 + L, Starving ≥ 70 (Nether ×0.75), Ravenous 100 (devours most devoted follower, public +10, reset 60), Sated < 30 (power ×1.1). Gorging = 5 crystals in one night → +1 Ichor (once per night).
- Malice: rise 3 + L, Seething ≥ 70 (odds −0.15, public +2/turn), Outburst 100 (atrocity in a random Active zone, all tracks +20, reset 40). Bitter followers vent ×1.5.
- **Watershed gate** = L4 **and** the Lift has been Active territory (or held by a follower with Devotion ≥ 50) at least once — sticky `flags.liftReached`. Level is capped at 4 in Whisper; reaching L4 + lift sets phase `watershed` and the sim stops.

**New rules (not in the spec)**
- **One whisper per person per night** (Influence on a target already whispered this night is illegal: "Already whispered to tonight"). Stops Nether-dumping one person to certainty.
- Conversion: on success the person joins at Devotion 12; failure lowers their Resistance by 8 and adds +2 public (as drafted). Personal Resistance rolled 10–26 plus trait/role terms; Kiln light near the shrine adds +15 (in the shrine) / +8 (adjacent) and drains follower Devotion 3/1 per dawn until desecrated or snuffed. Converting a kiln-lit person gives cult +4, a failed whisper there cult +3.
- **Carry-over**: unspent Nether carries at 50 %, capped at one turn's income. **Ember 8** flat Nether (approved draft).
- **Territory**: Control ≥ 50 claims a zone; a claimed zone whose Control falls below **15** first goes *slipping* (one-dawn grace, a warning event), then is lost (−2 Ichor) if still below. The Hollow is anchored (never lost).
- **Crystals**: the Hollow seeps 1/dawn; Smuggle (diggers) yields 1 (+1 greedy, +1 blessed) limited by each vein's **capacity** (Grave 1, Heart Vein 2, West Drift 1, East Workings 2, Low Gallery 1, Sump 1; Crystal Bloom +1) shared between smugglers in the zone; heat **2.5 government per crystal** × (1 − Cunning/150). Skim (sorters, Sorting Floor) yields 2 (+greedy, +blessed), heat **6** × (1 − Cunning/150). A death in the mine crystallizes the soul (+1 crystal, +2 if sacrificed). Start 5 crystals.
- **Suspicion pipeline per dawn**: orders resolve → drift (violet crests: +0.4 public per follower; public feeds cult and government by floor(public/25)) → thresholds fire → decay −2 each track. Thresholds 25/50/75/100 fire once; a track must drop 10 below a threshold to re-arm it. Quiet Rot multiplies gains by 0.75.
- **Whisper faction responses** (light pushback, per-stage responder registry; Shade registers its own): Government — inspections whenever ≥ 25 (chance 0.25 + gov/250; a clean inspection −2, a caught smuggler/skimmer is taken), L2 Auditor hunter, L3 **one overseer crackdown** (up to 3 followers taken, then gov −20), L4 Lockdown (4 dawns of raids). Cult — L1 vigils (Resistance +5 near the Kiln), L2 Cantor hunter (curdles Devotion where it walks), L3 Rite of Cleansing (burns one follower), L4 the Kindling (3 dawns of burnings in your Active zones). Public — L2 bolted doors (Resistance +6), L3 a mob, L4 a riot. Hunters move one zone per dawn toward your followers; Sabotage and Lie Low drive them off/hide from them.
- **Orders**: pray, scout, lieLow, smuggle, skim, preach (Kiln-Keepers, or anyone at Devotion ≥ 60), intimidate, shield (guards), and evil acts terrorize (D25), sabotage (diggers/guards, D35; blocks a zone 2 dawns, 35 % kill), sacrifice (D50), desecrate (Kiln-Keepers, D40; +25 XP, big cult heat), frame (D30, Cunning 45; target is taken, gov −12). Direct cost = `S^0.85 · T` = 1 Nether for a Mortal (formula exact; it is cheap by design — the price of an order is its heat and risk, not Nether). Cancelling refunds.
- **No dead turn**: if a night would start with nothing meaningful affordable, *Ember Flares* (+Nether) or *the Hollow Weeps* (+crystals) fires at dusk.
- **Events**: 20 Whisper events (10 with a 2-option choice), ≤ 1 per dawn at 60 % with per-event cooldowns: dream-talker, cave-in, tally inspection, conscription lists (war), death in the tunnels (soul crystallizes), Kiln feast, smuggler's offer, informer, omen, rail accident, double shift, sump cache, letters from the front, hollow hymn, overseer's ledger, the shaking, crystal fever, fresh hands (new named miner), begged blessing, war bells.
- **People**: 28 named Ghelm (g01–g28), g17 Vantreth Kol is the Overseer Elite (cannot be influenced in Whisper). Dead/taken units stay in `state.units` with `alive: false`, `follower: false`, `gone: reason`; `joinedTurn` keeps their history.

### P3 — Audio

All sound is WebAudio synthesis in `src/audio/` (no files, no libraries). One graph (`core.ts`) runs live (`AudioContext`, driven by a 90 ms timer that schedules 0.7 s ahead, 2.6 s while the tab is hidden) and offline (`OfflineAudioContext`, driven with explicit times) — the offline checks hear exactly what players hear.

**API** (`src/audio/index.ts`): `createAudio({ muted?, seed? }) → AudioEngine` with `unlock / setScene(scene, { fadeSeconds }) / setIntensity(0..1) / setHunger(0..1) / sfx(name, { pan, gain, pitch }) / setVolume / getVolume / setMuted / isMuted / suspend / resume / dispose`. It also exports `WebAudioEngine`, `MUSIC_SCENES`, `SFX_NAMES` and the types. The engine never throws. Without WebAudio it does nothing. It creates its `AudioContext` lazily on `unlock()`, and also unlocks itself on the first pointerdown, keydown or touchend, so the page never gets an autoplay warning. Scene, intensity and hunger set before unlock are applied when it unlocks. `?mute=1` mutes. Volumes and mute are kept in `localStorage['hungrygod.audio.v1']` (guarded). Integration: call `setScene` on every scene change, `setIntensity(max suspicion track / 100, or more when Ichor is low)` and `setHunger(hunger / 100)` each Dawn, and `sfx()` on sim events. `textBlip` is meant for cutscene text ticks; it is rate-limited to one every 30 ms.

**Mix and safety:** beds → music bus (cave IR 4.6 s RT60 and hall IR 2.9 s returns) → duck → master. Sfx → sfx bus (chamber IR 2.0 s and room IR 1.0 s). Master → DC-block highpass at 18 Hz → `DynamicsCompressor` (−9 dB, 6:1, 4 ms / 220 ms) → a WaveShaper limiter that is exactly linear up to 0.84 and can never exceed 0.965. Impulse responses are generated: decorrelated stereo noise, exponential decay, a lowpass that darkens the tail over time, sparse early reflections, unit energy, and an exact zero at the end. Noise buffers are seeded and loop-seamless. Every envelope starts and ends at exactly 0 (`params.envPoints`). Crossfades are 4-segment equal-power ramps that start with `cancelAndHoldAtTime`, so a fade can be interrupted mid-way without a step. Events scheduled after their time has passed are dropped, because a late envelope would click. Volume sliders follow a squared law (0.5 ≈ −12 dB). Defaults: master 0.85, music 0.8, sfx 0.9. Big sfx duck the music (stageUp/victory/defeat −10 dB, maliceOutburst −9, ravenous −8, crusadeHorn −7…). Anti-spam: each sfx has a minimum gap and there is a 24-voice cap that only low-priority sounds respect. Every play varies by about ±20 cents and ±0.6 dB, on top of each recipe's own jitter.

**Key, mode, motif.** Everything sits on **D** (D2 = 73.4 Hz). The mine is **D Phrygian**: the b2 (Eb) makes every descent sink. The town is **D Aeolian**: a natural 2nd opens it up. The ending is **D Phrygian dominant**: a major third over the b6 and b2, an ominous promise. **The Hungry One's motif is A–Bb–A–F–D** (degrees 5-b6-5-b3-1, rhythm 1-½-1½-1-3 beats). It is a sigh on the b6 that falls to the tonic, and its degrees are shared by every mode used, so it survives every scene. Variants (`theory.transformMotif`): fragment (the sigh A–Bb–A held), tail, inversion, answer (D–F–A–Bb–A, ends open), sequence, augment, ornament (passing b2 before the tonic), echo, **rising** (D–F–A–Bb–A–D′, the resolution) and **collapse** (keeps sinking below the tonic, slowing and going progressively flat, down to −110 cents).

**Generative system.** `MotifComposer`, `ChordComposer` and `GapStream` are seeded and chunk-invariant: asking for many small windows gives the same notes as one big window (unit-tested), so live and offline match. Melodies are built phrase by phrase from weighted motif variants, with a phrase-arc velocity, ±3 % timing humanisation, an optional third-below harmony line and long rests. **Sections** of 44–55 s cycle through flavours (sparse → statement → deep → develop → statement → high), which change variant weights, register (±12), density and harmony, so a bed develops over minutes. Chord centres move every 14–26 s in the mine and every 2-bar multiple in town. Slow LFOs at unrelated rates (drone breathing at 0.035 Hz and 0.095 Hz, wind at 0.03–0.15 Hz) mean nothing lines up into an audible loop.

**Beds** (loudness measured over 30 s offline, BS.1770 integrated, at default volumes; target −18 to −14 LUFS):
- `cutscene` (~−17.5 LUFS) is a timeline written for the five cutscene beats. 0–18 s: the void (cold high wind), a choir held on D5/A5/D6, star twinkles, the motif high on dark glass, then augmented an octave lower. 16–23 s: the fall, a warm-saw glissando from A5 down to A1 with a closing filter, a falling noise sweep, and a sub impact at 22.6 s. 20–40 s: burial, as the drone and rumble rise, distant picks begin (a civilisation builds), drips start, the motif sounds in the low register, and at 34 s a descending chain of crystal tones (souls hardening). 42–52 s: "reaches up and fails", a rising answer on bowed strings whose last note sags 30 cents, over a faint heartbeat. 52 s on: one thin high tone (the thread of shadow), then sparse fragments for as long as the scene stays.
- `bonus` (~−18): a held breath. A soft D pedal, a choir cluster held on A4+Bb4+D4 (the motif's sigh frozen and never resolved), filtered-noise inhale and exhale every 7.5–9 s, glass A→Bb sighs, rare shimmer.
- `whisper` (~−17): the mine. A D/A drone through a breathing lowpass, plus a rumble. Phrygian chord centres (i, bVI, iv, bII). Dripping water (sine "bubble" pitch sweeps plus splash ticks, ~3 s apart, panned, very wet in the cave IR). Distant pick strikes in clusters of 2–5 (noise crack, inharmonic steel ring at ratios 1/2.76/5.4, low thud, lowpassed, almost fully wet). Crystal shimmer (high sine pairs with vibrato). The melody is a low dark-glass mallet, sparse, 1.15 s beat (~52 BPM), with a warm glow pad only in the fuller sections. Measured layer balance: melody ≈ −21, drone ≈ −24, drips ≈ −29, shimmer ≈ −30, picks ≈ −31 LUFS.
- `watershed` (~−15): a rising swell. Pink and white noise swells grow exponentially from near silence. A tone climbs two octaves (D2→D4) over 11.4 s. The choir climbs Dm → F → Bb → C while the drone and its filter open up. At 11.5 s the bloom: a wide open D (D3-A3-E4-A4-D5) on choir and pad, a sub impact, two bells, and the **rising** motif on bowed strings. The town's wind comes in from 9 s. After 24 s an open, slowly turning field with answer/rising fragments high up.
- `shade` (~−16.5): the town of Sallowreach. Wind (stereo pink bands with gusts). A quieter drone. Formant **choir pads** (oo/ah/oh vowels morphing) on Aeolian progressions (i–bVI–bVII–i, i–iv–bVI–i…). A **slow pulse**: a soft frame drum on beats 1 and 3 at 50 BPM, with ghost notes. The melody is **bowed low strings**, with more motion than whisper (shorter rests, sequences, inversions, harmony thirds). A high glass echo of the motif remembers the mine. The **Kiln temple bells** toll in A3/Eb3 pairs (a tritone) every 14–48 s, panned right because the temple is bottom-right on the map. A distant crowd murmur.
- `ending` (~−15.5): D Phrygian dominant (D–Bb–Eb–D). The **rising** motif on bowed strings doubled by glass an octave up. At 14 s a distant gold bell answers with the sigh (A–Bb–A) from the right: the city's god has noticed. Then rising and answer phrases continue over a choir.
- `defeat` (~−18): the **collapse** motif on bowed strings and glass. The drone sags D→C→A while its filter closes (380→110 Hz) and its level drains. A heartbeat slows over nine beats and stops. The collapse is repeated an octave lower at 17 s, then sparse deep echoes and drips.
- **Intensity** (whisper/shade weight 1, watershed 0.4, story scenes 0) adds a low string tremolo on D/A/D, 4.5→10 Hz, whose root steps restlessly to Eb as tension rises (probability 0.1→0.7). Above 0.25 a low drum pulse joins at 64→104 BPM, with off-beat ticks above 0.7. At intensity 1 this layer is ≈ −19.6 LUFS on its own. **Hunger** adds a lub-dub heartbeat at 46→72 BPM, a saturated sub growl (41/43.7 Hz, irregular wobble), and stomach gurgles (resonant brown noise sweeping 90–330 Hz) at mean gaps of 14→5 s; on its own ≈ −19.5 LUFS. The growl uses a steeper curve than the heartbeat, so the heartbeat creeps in first.

**Sfx** (40). Each is layered attack/body/tail and calibrated to a peak target: UI ticks around −25 dBFS (`uiHover`, `textBlip`), UI clicks −13 to −18, game events −6 to −12, big moments −2.5 to −5. Every peak is under −2 dBFS, and the measured values are in `screenshots/audio/report.json`. Notable designs:
- `whisper` is sample-synthesised in JS (`synthbuf.ts`): four unvoiced formant syllables with diphthong motion, through a Schroeder reverb, reversed so the tail becomes an inhaled swell, over a low hollow tone.
- `convert` is a Dm(add9) warm-saw bloom opening to 2.4 kHz, with crystal sparkles and a sub. `convertFail` is two tones sliding apart into a dissonance, with a dull thud.
- `eatCrystal` is 5–7 crystal grains (inharmonic pings + noise grains) and a gulp (280→75 Hz). `gorge` is three crunch bursts, a gulp, and a satisfied low "ah→oo" groan.
- `miracle` is a rising noise swell into a bell + choir stab. `miracleEvil` is a swell into a saturated D–Ab–Eb tritone cluster with a closing filter and a sub boom.
- `crusadeHorn` is a reed and warm-saw horn through brass formants, with a 70-cent scoop and delayed vibrato. `thresholdAlarm` is a bright Kiln bell struck twice. `inquisitor` is three heavy footsteps and a low horn with a cold overtone.
- `stageUp` and `victory` quote the rising motif. `defeat` quotes the collapse. `levelUp` is a D–F–A–D glass arpeggio. `textBlip` is a 45 ms sine tick on a random Phrygian degree.

**Verification** (`node e2e/audio-check.cjs`, about 2.5 min, four pages rendering in parallel):
- Every sfx is rendered twice with different seeds. Each render must pass:
  - audible: peak ≥ 0.02 and RMS ≥ 0.002
  - no clipping: peak ≤ 0.98
  - no DC: offset < 0.002
  - no click at the start: the first audible sample is below 0.02
  - no click at the end: the last 10 ms before the −48 dB point stay below 10 % of the peak, and the render ends in exact silence
  - sane duration: 0.03–4 s, measured to the −48 dB point
- **Distinctness:** for every one of the 780 sfx pairs, the fingerprint distance must exceed both 0.05 and 1.5 × the larger play-to-play variation of the two sfx. The fingerprint is 12 log-band levels, 16 × 0.2 s envelope slices, 8 × 25 ms attack slices, centroid and duration.
- Every scene is rendered for 30 s at its base settings, with intensity 1 and with hunger 1. Each render must pass:
  - never silent: every 1 s window after the fade-in is above −49 dBFS
  - no clipping and no DC
  - loudness between −24 and −12 LUFS
  - evolves: the 1 s RMS has a standard deviation ≥ 0.4 dB
- The danger layers must change the whisper and shade fingerprints. The scenes must differ pairwise. `silence` must be silent.
- **Crossfades:** seven real transitions are rendered. In each, the largest sample step inside the crossfade window must be ≤ 1.25 × the steady-state maximum, and the 100 ms RMS must never drop by more than half of the silence floor.
- The live engine must run, its analyser after the limiter must show signal, and the console must stay free of errors.
- Outputs go to `screenshots/audio/`: a spectrogram per scene and variant, a crossfade spectrogram, `sfx-contact-sheet.png`, `audio-bench.png` and `report.json`.
- Pure parts are unit-tested in `tests/audio/`: modes and motif variants, composer chunk-invariance and section development, envelope math, parameter maps, FFT, BS.1770 (a 997 Hz sine reads −3.01 LUFS), impulse responses, noise seams, WAV encoding and the limiter curve.

### Orchestrator — user overrides
- **Fog edges (user, binding):** no visible band of any kind at a fog boundary — no glowing rim between colour and gray, no pale halo between gray and void. Active→Sensed is a seamless organic falloff of colour and light; Sensed→Void sinks smoothly into pure black. Light comes only from real light sources. This overrides the "glowing color/gray edge" wording wherever it would produce a visible strip.
- **Close-up quality (user, binding):** the mine (and later the town) must look illustrated at zoom 2, not only in overview.
- **Scope (user):** build through the end of the town (Shade). Nothing past the town except the short ending beat that teases the city.

### P2 — Mine render
- **User direction (binding, overrides the earlier brief):** (1) *No visible band on any fog boundary* — no rim, halo, outline or strip. Active→Sensed is a seamless organic falloff where colour and light drain to gray over a soft irregular distance; Sensed→Void is gray sinking smoothly into pure black. Light comes only from real sources (crystals, torches, Kiln shrine, Tally lamp, god's pulse), never from the fog edge. (2) Close-up (zoom 1.8–2.5) must hold illustrated quality: form and light on every object, varied rock masses, layered floors, detailed props, faceted glowing crystals, Ghelm designed at close range.
- **World & palette:** mine world is 2400×1400 world units (`mine/layout.ts`); zones keep the 12 binding ids of §6 and exactly the §6 tunnels. Palette in `art/palette.ts`: ink `#0b0d10`; earth `#6e5236`; rock families teal `#3d6766` / ochre `#9a7442` / gray `#5d6468` / violet `#4e4762` / slate `#48545f` (each with light/dark/top; island tops lean teal, e.g. `#25585a`); crystal `#7046c8`→`#eadfff`; Kiln gold `#ffb340`; Tally steel-blue `#7fa8d8`; crests neutral `#a6e6da`, follower `#b47cff`, kiln `#ffc54d`, tally `#86afe8`; lightmap ambient `#7c8592`; void `#000`.
- **Layers:** static art is baked once into an *albedo* canvas at `BAKE_SCALE = 2` px per world unit (sharp to zoom ≈2.5) and a *gray* (Sensed) canvas at `GRAY_SCALE = 1` (albedo × static lightmap, desaturated, low contrast), each with a halving mip chain picked per zoom. During the bake a light field (`setBakeLight`/`lightAt` in `art/draw.ts`, built by `bakeLightField` in `mine/bake.ts`) gives every stone, pebble, plank and barrel a lit side facing the nearest light and a cast shadow away from it. Per frame: albedo → × dynamic lightmap (half-res buffer, multiply) → people (unlit, so they read) → emissive (bloom, flames, crests, glints, dust, veins) ; then the gray buffer (gray layer + gray silhouettes, black where Void, cut away where Active) is composited on top; then haze, zone affordances, person markers, effects, far point. The gray buffer is cached and only rebuilt when the camera, fog masks or (4 Hz) gray people change.
- **Fog (`fog.ts`, generic for the town):** per fog cell (8 world units) Active amount *a* and Seen amount *s*. Zone membership is soft: each zone's floor cells are blurred (near + far blur) into weights, each cell keeps its 3 strongest zones, sampled through a domain warp — so fronts bend round rock and are never straight Voronoi/BFS cuts. A noisy threshold makes fronts irregular; *a* drains over a longer distance than *s*. Fades creep out from the zone anchor (or recede to it) in 1.1 s. Masks only change during fades; the screen-blended overlay holds only a faint drifting haze over Sensed ground. No rim/halo/glow code exists any more.
- **Zone affordances:** no outlines; a soft inner glow following the zone's floor (brightest just inside its edge), only when `selectable` (faint breathing lavender), `highlighted` (pale) or `selected` (violet). `control` = faint violet tint + creeping vein lines.
- **People:** each person gets a deterministic spot in their zone by role (diggers at crystal faces, sorters at tables, haulers on the rails…; never in a pool). When `zoneId` changes they walk along the tunnel centrelines (BFS over the zone graph) at 95 units/s. Their visibility is sampled from the fog at their feet, so walkers fade across fronts. Crest colour cross-fades in 0.9 s on allegiance change. Ghelm: segmented carapace with rim light, three eyes with glints, whiskers, mandibles, clawed hands gripping role tools, harness by role, glowing crest fins + filaments; Sensed = dim gray silhouettes.
- **Far zoom:** below camera scale ≈0.3 the map sinks under a black veil and condenses into a violet star (glow + slow 8-ray flare) at the crystal heart — the hook for the Watershed transition.

### P5 — App shell + Whisper UI
- **Shell:** one canvas letterboxed to 16:9, backing store = CSS size × devicePixelRatio, context pre-scaled to 1600×900 logical units; `#ui` is sized to the same box and carries `--u` (CSS px per logical unit). Every DOM size is `calc(var(--u) * N)`, so the HUD scales with the art and stays crisp. `#ui` and scene roots use `overflow: clip` so focus/scrollIntoView can never shift the HUD.
- **Scenes:** registry (`src/app/registry.ts`) + `import.meta.glob('./scenes/*/index.ts')` in main: a piece adds a scene by creating its `index.ts`; real scenes replace placeholders whatever the import order. Each scene gets its own root div inside `#ui` (removed on exit). Transitions: `fade` (UI fades out while the old scene still renders, then the canvas cross-fades from a snapshot of its last frame), `black`, `cut`. A leaving scene cannot redirect the flow.
- **Hand-over without a cut:** the mine renderer is shared (`getMineRenderer()`, art seed fixed at 1 — the mine looks the same every run; the run seed drives only the sim). `whisperStartCamera()` frames grave + heartvein + cartway exactly like `dev/mine.html?preset=start`. The cutscene passes `{ world: { renderer, camera, view } }` through bonus to whisper, which reuses them and fades the HUD in over the drawn map.
- **Session:** owns seed + action log (= full replay), publishes events, autosaves to `localStorage` after every action (guarded; cleared on defeat). Continue replays the log; a save whose action became illegal after a sim change stops at that point instead of failing.
- **Sim adapter:** every app/UI/scene module imports the sim through `src/app/sim.ts` only. Menus, costs, odds, blockers and texts come from the sim (`orderMenu`, `miracleHand`, `conversionOddsFor`, `actionBlocker`, `describeAction`, breakdown selectors). Unknown future event kinds are narrated from their `text`; unknown legal action types are offered as generic "Other actions" buttons (labelled by `describeAction`) in the inspector.
- **Whisper layout (logical px):** top bar 62 tall (Divinity, Ichor pips, Nether, Crystals, Faith, Presence, Hunger, Malice, Influence, Suspicion ×3, Night, settings); objectives strip top-left; chronicle under it (collapsible, remembered); inspector right (352 wide, only when something is selected); miracle hand bottom-centre peeking 112 px and lifting on hover; Eat + End Night bottom-right. Zone names are drawn on the canvas in the display serif, fading with visibility (none in Void), a violet diamond marks territory.
- **Crest colours on the map:** follower violet, Kiln-Keepers gold, guards/overseers (and any non-Mortal) Tally steel, everyone else neutral teal. Followers at Devotion ≥ 70 carry the `apostle` marker (they will stay named at the watershed); hunters (Auditor/Cantor) are drawn as Ghelm with the `inquisitor` marker.
- **Night feedback:** a whisper first flies (thread from the god), and the result lands ~1 s later (crest flips, convert/convertFail effect, toast) — the crest change is held back by a display override so the reveal is not spoiled.
- **Dawn:** End Night replays the returned events as beats on the *pre-dawn* state with display overrides (people walk, crests turn, the dead leave, hunters move, zones light up), narrated in a caption; consecutive moves merge into one beat; the camera glances at off-screen action. Space skips (remaining beats apply instantly; level-up/threshold/ichor/lift banners still show). Then a Dawn report (followers ±names, Devotion avg, crystals, Nether income + carry, Suspicion ×3, XP/level, Hunger, Malice, Ichor, new hand), then any story event as a modal (1–2 options with the sim's hints; keys 1/2).
- **Banners** queue (never overlap). **Toasts** stack 3 deep. Disabled buttons are never inert: clicking one plays the denied sound and toasts the reason; hovering shows it.
- **Onboarding hints** (violet bubbles, one at a time, remembered in `localStorage hg.hints.v1`, re-enable from settings): first whisper, first follower → orders, first castable miracle, Hunger ≥ 50, first Suspicion ≥ 25, End Night on night 1.
- **Keys:** Space/Enter end night / skip dawn / continue; W whisper; 1–3 miracle; E eat; Tab next follower; C chronicle; F frame territory; arrows pan; Esc cancel targeting → close hint → deselect → settings. Right-click cancels targeting; double-click focuses a zone.
- **Audio:** intensity = max(highest Suspicion/100, 0.9 × missing Ichor fraction); hunger layer = (Hunger − 40)/60.
- **Defeat epitaph** picks its text by the last Ichor-loss reason (followers taken / territory lost / Rite of Unbinding / default).
- **e2e isolation:** `e2e/whisper-shots.cjs` stubs Vite's `/@vite/client` so concurrent edits by other pieces cannot hot-reload the page mid-run.
- **e2e bot:** the playthrough uses the sim author's own `cautious` bot, lifted from `scripts/sim-smoke.ts` and transpiled at run time, so the UI run tracks rule changes; a small local policy is the fallback. The defeat screenshot comes from random play over seeds until one is lost.
- **Space safety:** Space/Enter cannot end a night within 0.7 s of it starting, so the key press that closes the dawn report never also skips the next night.
- **Tooltips in side panels** open beside the panel (`data-tip-side="left"`) so they never hide the list they explain; a tooltip whose target slides away (`.away`/`.hidden`) closes.
- **Dawn report** sits at y 268 so level-up / Suspicion banners (top band) never overlap it.
- **`?mute=1`** mutes that page load only (`createAudio({ muted })`); only the settings toggle persists mute.
- **Hand-over fidelity:** Whisper keeps a handed camera untouched and continues `view.time` (world clock offset), so the first gameplay frame equals the cutscene's last; every hop of the chain uses transition `cut`. The title starts the sliced mine bake (`preloadMine`).
- **External libraries, tools and assets (user, binding — replaces the "no external assets" rule in §0):** allowed whenever the licence permits the use and the result passes our criteria. This lifts the earlier bans on libraries, image/audio/font files and PixiJS. Conditions: record every dependency, tool and asset with its source and licence in `CREDITS.md`; keep the sim core dependency-free, deterministic and headless; nothing may be fetched from the network at runtime (bundle it); a builder that adds an npm dependency runs the install itself, one at a time, and notes it in its handoff.

### P7 — Cutscene
- **Shape:** 74 s, Canvas 2D at 1600×900, every frame a pure function of the cutscene time `t` (seekable; `dev/cutscene.html?t=&speed=&mute=1&fast=1`). Paced to the P3 `cutscene` bed: the void and choir (0–18), the fall's glissando and the sub impact at 22.6, the burial and distant picks (20–40), the crystal chain at 34, "reaches up and fails" (42–52), the thin thread tone from 52. The bed is restarted when the scene's first frame plays, so its timeline lines up with `t = 0`.
- **Beats and shots** (`script.ts` `BEATS`):
  - **Throne** 0–15.4 (shot A, `shotThrone.ts`). A robed god with one vertical violet eye and a crown of night-glass shards on a floating throne among stars. The Flame's gold lance strikes at 13.85 and the throne shatters at 15.3.
  - **Fall** 15.4–23.6 (shot B, `shotFall.ts`). A violet comet falls over an alien night landscape (two moons, stalk-eyed flora, rib arches) and impacts at 22.6, with a white-out and a shockwave. Shots A and B cross-dissolve from 16.7.
  - **Ages** 23.6–34 (shot C, `shotStrata.ts` + `strataArt.ts`). A cross-section of the grave. Dust clears on the smoking crater, and young beds settle into it as days and nights flicker past: the sun streaks across the sky once per day, smeared into an arc, and clouds race. Flora and grave-stones come first, then the miners' settlement rises out of the ground: ribbed domes, swollen spires, and the Flame's temple of stacked discs with a gold sigil built right over the grave. A headframe sinks a shaft and galleries branch, while war glows on the horizon. The camera rises toward the surface as the settlement is built.
  - **Souls** 34–42. Teal souls sink from the graves, the temple and the war through the stone. Where they reach the hollow they harden into violet crystal around the god. The camera sinks with them.
  - **Reach** 42–52.6. A thick arm of shadow strains up through the rock, cracking it, toward the lowest gallery's gold lamp. It trembles and the camera shakes, then at 48.3 the hand fails. The arm sags, crumbles and dissolves into ash. The camera dives into the god's eye, which closes into black at 52.25.
  - **Thread** 52.6–74 (shot G, `shotMine.ts`). Drawn by the **real mine renderer**. A violet slit opens in the black: the eye-shaped fissure in the god's grave (`grave`). The darkness lifts outward from it in a soft, irregular iris that only ever darkens, never forming a rim. At 55 one thread of shadow slips out along the tunnel floor, west into the rail junction (`cartway`) and then north and east toward the lift. The rail junction turns Sensed at 60.2 through the renderer's own fog fade. A shorter filament makes the crystal gallery (`heartvein`) Sensed at 60.9, then draws back. The camera eases from a close-up (scale 2.35) to exactly `whisperStartCamera()` between 58.6 and 71. From 72.5 the frame holds with nothing drawn but the renderer.
- **Names (§13.3):** no on-screen line uses a name; the god is only "I", its Hunger only "we"; nothing is labelled in the world.
- **God alone:** no person appears anywhere. The settlement is buildings and lights only, with no figures.
- **Voices** (`text.ts`): a lower third with a soft vignette, typewriter reveal at 34 cps with a per-character fade, and `textBlip` ticks every other character.
  - **The god:** violet, upright serif.
  - **Its own Hunger:** bone-ochre italic with a small `HUNGER` tag. Hunger always says "we".
- **Lines (13):**
  - "Once, I sat a throne of night, and the stars kept their distance." / "We ate the ones that didn't."
  - "My kin broke the throne, and threw me down."
  - "I fell a long way. I lay a long time." / "Long enough for strangers to build on our grave. And dig."
  - "Their dead sink through the stone, and harden around me." / "A feast, packed all around us. Just out of reach."
  - "I am a god. I will take what is mine." / "Look at us. We can't even lift a hand."
  - "Then not a hand. A thread." / "Up the shafts. Find one who will listen."
  - "One is enough to begin." / "Everyone starts small."
- **Sound cues (8):**
  - 13.85 `thresholdAlarm`: the gold lance
  - 15.3 `purge`: the throne shatters
  - 35.9 / 37.4 / 39.2 `soulCrystallize`
  - 48.35 `convertFail`: the reach fails
  - 55.2 `whisper`: the thread
  - 60.2 `reveal`: the rail junction is sensed
  - Cues fire only while playing forward through them, never on a seek.
- **Bake strategy:**
  - `enter` starts the shared mine bake in the background (`preloadMineRenderer(1, 8 ms)` via `world.ts`).
  - The scene's own art (throne, fall, strata ≈ 2 s) is baked behind a held black first frame.
  - The renderer is taken (`getMineRenderer()` + `whisperStartCamera()`) at t ≥ 45 once its bake is done. Otherwise it is taken at t = 52.28 in the black between the eye closing and the fissure opening; taking it finishes any remaining bake synchronously. A seek, skip, `?fast=1` or finish past that point also takes it.
- **No-cut hand-over:**
  - On finish or skip the scene calls `goto('bonus', { ...incomingData, world: { renderer, camera, view } }, { transition: 'cut' })`.
  - `renderer` is the shared `MineRenderer` instance and `camera` is the very `Camera` instance the last frame used, sitting exactly on its targets at the Whisper start framing.
  - `view` is the last frame's `WorldView`: `grave` active, `cartway` and `heartvein` sensed, the rest void, no people, `control 0`, nothing selectable. Its `time = 121.4` is the renderer clock, which is t − 52.6 + 100. `godPulse` is 0.55 + 0.25·sin(0.8·time), the Whisper formula.
  - Drawing that renderer with that camera and view gives the cutscene's last frame bit for bit (e2e: max diff 0).
  - Types: `CutsceneWorld`, `CutscenePayload` in `src/scenes/cutscene/index.ts`.
- **What the next scenes must NOT do:**
  - re-settle or re-set the fog, or call `settle()` (a fade's end state differs slightly from `settle()` in the renderer);
  - re-fit, snap or clamp the camera on entry;
  - reset `view.time`. Continue the clock from `view.time` (Whisper does: `viewT0`).
  - Zone affordances, territory tint and people may then fade in through the renderer's own easing; that is the HUD/world fading in over the same picture, not a cut.
- **Skip:**
  - Esc skips at once. Space, Enter, a click or mouse movement first reveals a small "Skip ESC" pill (bottom right), and Space or Enter then skips.
  - The skip dips to black over 0.35 s, seeks to 74 while the screen is black, and fades in over 0.7 s onto the final frame, then hands over.
  - After a jump, `MineShot.converge()` renders off screen until the renderer's full-res Sensed composite (recomposed 8 tiles/frame after a fog change) has caught up. `finalize()` also rebuilds the fog haze at exactly the final clock. So skip from any timestamp and `?fast=1` land on the identical frame (e2e: max diff 0 at 3/20/40/55/70 s and fast); a real-time playthrough lands within 1/255 of it.
  - `?fast=1` resolves straight to the hand-over.

#### Round 2 (P7 fix round after critic r1 6.5/10)
- **One god, one model sheet** (`cutscene/god.ts`): hooded night-mantle, long skull-mask, two back-swept horns, a circlet of three night-glass shards (one snapped), ONE level almond eye with a vertical violet slit (the shape of the grave's fissure), gaunt four-fingered claws. The throne, the falling sprite, the burial body + head and the close-up all draw this head; the throne no longer has its own. The buried mantle's folds hang under gravity from the back to a smooth hem (fanned folds read as wing ribs / shell plates).
- **The thread** (`render/mine/thread.ts`, §13.6) is the cutscene's last image and stays in play: `drawGodThread(ctx, camera, { time: view.time, reach: 1, rest: 1 })` right after the renderer, every frame (payload `world.thread`). Its soft veil/halo are drawn at quarter resolution and blitted only over the strand's screen box (bilinear) — about half the cost of the earlier full-screen blits.
- **No background mine bake during the shots.** The bake (P2's sliced preload; its longest indivisible steps are 40–190 ms here) is finished behind the cutscene's held first frame; the title has usually preloaded most of it. That held frame is not plain black: it shows the god's last spark, which stays while the stars fade in and becomes the eye when it opens on the throne.
- **Cost:** soft, heavily overlapping layers that gain nothing from full resolution are drawn at half resolution: the impact's billowing dust and everything behind the head in the deep dive (camera scale ≥ 3; the head and eye stay full-res). Fully black frames skip the shot. Skip/seek convergence uses the renderer's public `converged` / `flushComposite(false)`.
- **Pause:** the shell stops calling `update` while its settings panel is open, so timeline, typewriter and cues hold. The score is a fixed timeline on the audio clock, so the cutscene suspends the audio engine while paused and resumes it after (picture and score stay in step; the panel's own clicks are silent meanwhile — the engine drops sfx while suspended, never queues them).
- **Art:** strata rock repainted as broad bedded planes with fine lamination and broken fractures (the small even facets read as paving); the fall's trail is one gradient polygon per layer (the per-chunk alphas read as stair steps); the cloud streak across the big moon is feathered; the iris opens briskly after the slit (shorter near-black hold).
- **Round 2, successor:** the thread is a slimmer tendril (body half-width 5.9 world units at the fissure, tapering), so it reads as a thread, not a pipe, and stays clearly visible at the start framing. In the burial shot, once the camera has sunk below the surface the sky, far town, war and surface buildings are not drawn (the opaque rock hides them); the fully fed body is one bake, not two; the eye light is drawn only over its lit box. The heaviest beat's draw fell by about a quarter. The real-time frame check runs twice, each run with its own blank-canvas control, and judges each beat on its best run: on this shared machine one run alone measures the load, not the cutscene (a 1 ms throne frame showed an 83 ms p95).

#### Round 3 (P7 — the opening retold in the theme; owner §19: "get something in place")
- **Story (40 s, 5 lines):** memory 0–9 s — a colossus of violet crystal with one vertical eye stands over the bowl, every wick on the terraces violet, wickfolk in front with their arms raised to it (inked, paper grain, vignette = a remembered picture); the Flame 9–15.4 — gold light (never a figure, theme §8) rises behind the far ridge and spreads as a ragged tide over sky and land, every wick it reaches gutters and relights gold, the people turn to it, the colossus drains and cracks with gold in the cracks, then shatters; the fall 15.4–22.6 — one shard (the eye) falls through the dark past the gold-lit land, into the shaft under a stalk-timber headframe, past a gold shrine nook and a white shard-lamp, through the shaft floor and on through solid rock, lit only by itself; the grave 22.6–40 — the real mine renderer: a violet burst at the fissure, a sealed pocket of light round the crystal mass, the eye barely opens, picks far off (the bed's), the thread goes out, the rail junction and the crystal gallery turn Sensed, the camera settles on the Whisper start view and holds.
- **Lines:** "Once, every light in this land was mine." / "Then the Flame came, and they turned to it." / "I broke. I fell. I lay in the dark." / (Hunger) "And we are so hungry." / "Listen. Someone is digging."
- **The shard is one object:** drawn in screen space over the memory, the dissolve and the descent (`shard.ts`), landing where the grave's fissure sits in the mine close-up.
- **Opening continuity:** the held first frame is a spark at FRONT's `OPENING_SPARK` (801.2, 356.4); it is the colossus' eye, which opens and pulls back into the memory.
- **Mine bake (orchestrator: never show black after Begin):** the cutscene starts at once on its own art; the mine's sliced bake (from the title) keeps running between frames and is never forced (it can wait on a worker). Only the last shot needs it: if it is not done at 22.05 s the timeline waits on a living frame (the shard breathing in the dark rock, the score held in step) and goes on when the bake is done; a skip meanwhile lands on that hold and completes by itself.
- **Reduce motion** (`settings.reduceMotion`): no camera shake, the colossus' pieces fade instead of flying, no shard trail, softer shatter and landing flashes; same timeline and final state.
- **Cue points:** `CUE_POINTS` in `script.ts` (spark 0, flameRises 9.3, shatter 14.55, fallStart 15.4, shaftFloor 20.9, impact 22.6, eyeStirs 25.2, picks 26, thread 29, sensed 31.2, settle 37.4, end 40); one-shots in `CUES` (ids flame-rises, colossus-shatters, shaft-floor, eye-stirs, thread, sensed). The current `cutscene` bed's own impact (22.6) and picks (26) already line up.
- Retired: the throne, the robed god, the alien fall, the strata burial (`shotThrone/shotFall/fallGod/shotStrata/strataArt/god.ts` deleted).

#### Round 3 (P2 mine render — builders #3/#4)
- **Affordances:** no outline/rim/edge profile. A zone's tint footprint = its *soft, warped fog membership weight* (the same field the fog fronts use) × the cells `zoneAt` can answer for (floor, wall faces, 3-cell rim), feathered by a short blur, then × seen² from the fog per cell (`footTint`, cached per fog version). So the tint thins out along the same organic front as the fog between zones (no straight owner cut) and dies exactly where the ground sinks into the black. Selectable = faint breathing lavender, hover = pale lavender, selected = violet. Measured: pixels where the fog's seen = active = 0 are #000 except a ≤ 2/255 bilinear fringe one fog cell wide.
- **Fog/composite performance:** the Sensed/Void layer is a world-space composite (`comp` full res + `compLo` half res) recomposed only in 96-unit fog tiles whose mask values moved (tolerance 3/255 mid-fade, exact when settled). Fades recompose `compLo` only (the stale full-res tiles show its upscaled result); full res catches up 8 tiles per frame once the fog is still. `comp` is seeded from `compLo` on the first composite so a stale tile is never transparent. No screen-space gray buffer, no periodic rebuild; gray people/glints/flames are drawn per frame faded by s·(1−a). Fog fronts are wider and wavier: zone membership blend 5 cells with a softer exponent (`FogSource.sharp`, mine 1.5), a/s blur radii 3/2, plus a fine warp octave.
- **Async bake API:** `preloadMineRenderer(seed, budgetMs)` → `MineRendererLoad` (progress, done, renderer, promise, hurry): the only way to get a mine renderer (no synchronous bake; a scene that needs it awaits the load, `app/worlds` `getMineRenderer()` throws before it is done). The bake is a generator job (`slice.ts`) run one slice per displayed frame, just after paint; each unit forces its own raster (1×1 drawImage snapshot — no getImageData readback, so no console warning). `renderer.flushComposite(settleFog)` + `renderer.converged` give callers a deterministic final frame.
- **Sensed luminance rule:** Sensed gray = 15 + 0.42·L (cool, lifted ink = lower line contrast) × static lightmap, then the screen-blended haze. Per-zone floor luminance Sensed/Active ≈ 0.56–0.72 (target 0.6–0.7) with saturation 0.09 vs 0.14–0.51 Active. Dim wet southern zones get a per-zone gain on their own light sources (`ZONE_LIGHT_GAIN`: sump 1.45, low gallery 1.35, east workings 1.2, lift 1.1). The crystal heart keeps a faint lavender trace when Sensed on purpose (the god's crystals are never fully gray).
- **Ground types:** per zone (`layout.ground`): haul (packed ochre, worn paths + cart ruts), crystal (cold teal-gray), damp (dark teal stone, more puddles), work (gray-brown with planking), quarters (red-brown), office (slate flagstones), shrine (soot-dark, kiln-brick), hollow (violet-gray), gallery (neutral gray). Puddles have depth, a lit rim and the reflection of the nearest light. A raised rock rim (rim top + boulders) runs round every void-facing cave edge; rock islands have top relief.
- **Effects:** eight distinct animations with lead-in and aftermath (`effects.ts`). The whisper is a living thread of shadow: soft outer veil, dense body, inky core, violet inner glow with travelling light beats, smoke peeling off, and a thinner braided second strand; tendrils curl round the target, then a crest pulse. Death: a larger pale soul with a violet aura.
- **Crests:** allegiance is carried by hue *and* shape. Fins keep their hue (little white); the additive halo uses a saturated hue per allegiance (neutral teal-green, follower violet growing with devotion + a hot core above 50, Kiln amber, Tally deep blue). Shape cues via `GhelmPaint.kind`: Tally = riveted steel collar with a square blue badge; Kiln = burning ember brand on the brow.
- **Flags:** warded = up to three bronze censers hung over the largest chambers (chain, smoke, light pool) + rising gold motes + a faint gold wash over the footprint; blocked = a tall scree cave-in with splintered props at the mouth of the zone's tunnel toward the grave (computed renderer-side from the tunnels), sized to the tunnel, with dust billows and trickling grit; bloom = extra crystal clusters at the zone's glints, glowing, with sparkles; desecrated shrine turns violet (unchanged).
- **Far zoom:** the point gets a slow breathing violet halo and a dozen motes drifting outward.
- **Chamber density (density builder, merged):** dense dressing lives in `art/props2.ts` (same language as props.ts: lit base + shadow side + lit edge from `lightAt`, contact shadow, heavy ink outline, thinner interior lines, construction/wear detail). The Kiln shrine is set 30 units forward of the north wall in a wider outcrop on a 3-step dais; a kiln-brick fan radiates from it (soot near the flame); prayer mats kneel toward it in rows either side of an aisle; a broad gold floor light (r 300, 0.7) pools over the fan. Votive candle layout is seeded from the cluster's position (`votiveFlames`) so dressing knows every animated candle flame without drawing. Sump: pump on the east pool's bank → pipe → trough; a duckboard over the bog between the two big pools links the north floor to the south ledge, where the hidden cache (§6) is half-buried; only the punt, the mooring posts and a half-sunk barrel stand in the water, by design. Every worker spot is hand-placed beside the thing its role works (facing it); fallback lattice spots skip pools.
- **Dependencies:** none added.

#### Round 2 (P1 rework: economy, pressure, stage-as-data; tuned with `scripts/sim-tune.ts`, confirmed at 200 seeds)
All numbers live in `src/sim/balance.ts` unless noted. This overrides the P1 numbers above where they differ.
- **Correction:** the Whisper stage has **12** choice events (not 10).
- **XP thresholds:** L2 90, **L3 210, L4 400** (`formulas.ts`), L5+ unchanged. XP: new follower **7** (group: 1 per 4 heads), Devotion marks 25/50/75/100 → **3/6/10/16** (× heads/4 for groups), zone first claimed **12**, evil = vented Malice **/2**, eat 1, desecrate 25, miracle = cost. **Deep Roots** pays the Heart Vein's 12 zone XP at the start.
- **Ichor:** the god wakes **wounded at 8** (`startIchor`; max still 10 + 2(L−1)); each **level-up closes 1** (`levelUpHeal`, IchorGainReason `levelUp`). No Dawn can tear more than **3** Ichor (`ichorLossCapPerDawn`; no one-night wipe-outs). Losing a follower: 1; at Devotion ≥ 50 **2** (`devotedLossAt`, `devotedLossIchor`); groups 1 per 10 heads. A **fledgling** (Devotion never reached 25) that merely lapses costs **no** Ichor. Territory: a zone slips for **3** dawns (`slipGraceDawns`) before it is lost (−1); a claim lost before it **took root (5 dawns, `zone.claimedTurn`)** costs nothing. Ichor still only falls through lost Influence or enemy weapons (the Outburst costs no Ichor directly).
- **Hunger:** rises 4 + L + 1 per 4 follower heads; crystal −10; Starving ≥ 70 = Nether ×0.6, no hum, −1 Devotion per follower per Dawn; Ravenous 100 devours the most devoted follower, resets to **70**. Gorge: first needs 5 crystals in one night, each later one **+3 more**, then **4 nights** cooldown. Hoard: crystals above 10 add +1 cult per 5 per Dawn.
- **Malice:** rises 4 + L; +3 when an enemy takes/kills/burns a follower; Seething ≥ 70 (odds −0.15, public +3/Dawn). **Outburst** (100) breaks out where your flock is thickest: each person there dies with chance **0.55**, surviving followers −40 health / −15 Devotion, all tracks **+15**, Malice resets to **40**, and it pays **no XP** (`outburstXpMult` 0).
- **People/odds:** odds use Resistance / **200** (`oddsResistDivisor`; draft /100); wariness **+3.5** Resistance per follower head beyond 2 (cap 100); Kiln vigils +5 × cult level (max 3).
- **Prices:** an order costs `OrderDef.price × Direct × 1.5^(L−1)`; Pray costs `2 / gainRate(D)` × Direct × 1.5^(L−1). Prices: scout 1, **lieLow 1**, smuggle 2, skim 3, preach 3, intimidate 2, shield 2, terrorize 3, sabotage 4, sacrifice 4, desecrate 6, frame 4.
- **Lie Low:** hides the follower from inspections, hunters and purges with chance **0.7** per Dawn (`lieLowCover`; Shield stays certain). Cooling: first −2 hottest / −1 public, each more half as much, capped −4 / −2 a Dawn.
- **Veins** hold 6 × richness crystals (Bloom +3), then are worked out (`veinExhausted`).
- **Suspicion drift per Dawn:** crests **+0.42** public per follower head; quota +0.25 government per head; Kiln-lit crests +1 cult per head; each miracle +1 cult +1 public; public feeds cult and government by public × 0.05. Cooling `max(0.5, 2 − 0.5(L−1))`.
- **Pushback:** inspections as before (taken on the 2nd catch, `inspectStrikes`); crackdown (gov 75) takes each exposed follower with chance **0.45**, max 4, once **per stage** (`flags.crackdownStage`); while a track is ≥ 75 it strikes with chance **0.7**, **at most 2 strikes a Dawn** (hottest tracks first), each strike killing/taking/burning with chance **0.6**; mob 2 victims × 60 harm. The **Cantor's song** (−10, pious −15) never makes a follower lapse on its own. **Reaching the gate is seen:** the first time the Lift is reached, government **+15** and cult **+10** (`gateAlarmGov/Cult`, narrated by `StageDef.gateAlarm`, factionAction `gateSeen`).
- **Bonuses:** Quiet Rot **−10 %** Suspicion gains (`BONUS_NUMBERS.suspicionMult` 0.9); Deep Roots also seeps **1 crystal every 3 dawns**; texts derive their numbers.
- **Stage data:** `content/stages.ts` (`StageDef`) holds every stage-specific text and id (gate, end, defeat, dusk, level texts, hunters, hints, cross texts, skim zones, stationary roles, crackdown leader, cave-in zones, hideout, arrivals, gate alarm). A second stage plugs in as data (tested with a fake stage).
- **Bots** (`scripts/sim-smoke.ts`, tuned with `scripts/sim-tune.ts`): rush, cautious, devotion, evil, clean (vents gently at Malice 65), reckless, neverEat (no eating, no Marrow Feast), neverVent (no evil at all), massLieLow, random. Results at 200 seeds: `handoffs/P1-smoke.txt`. Decent bots reach the watershed 93–97 % at medians 20–22 (p90 25–26); reckless loses 69 %, neverEat 96 %, neverVent 40 %, massLieLow 35 % (and slower, median 26), random 91 %; late unspent Nether 20–37 %; no dead turns; every bonus 58–70 % across all bots (90–99 % for decent bots).

#### Round 4 (P2 mine render — art track B, refined vector style; builder #5)
- **Rock language (replaces the boulder piles, §13.5):** rock is one continuous body of *fitted blocks* (`art/rockface.ts`): a Voronoi tiling of block centres (no gaps, no overlaps), each block cut into an unevenly inset top plane and bevel planes that turn away from it; every plane gets a flat stepped tone (shadow / half-tone / lit / highlight, soft quantisation) from how its normal faces the bake light, and its lit end takes the light's own hue (`BakeLight.hue`, additive: gold by the shrine, violet by crystals, warm by torches) while shadow planes stay cool. Fine hatching on the darkest planes only; ink is broken and weighted by shade on the creases between blocks (heavier in shadow, gaps where lit), faint on folds inside a block; the whole tiling is domain-warped so every edge wavers like a hand line yet stays shared by both blocks.
- **Cave rock (`mine/rock.ts`):** the rock top (rim + islands) is blocks seen from above, sloping toward the floor at the cave edge (lit lips) and turning darker, calmer and into the family's deep top colour inside big masses (fewer, fainter creases); the north cliff faces are blocks in rough courses (wider than tall), lit tops, dark undercuts, darker at the foot. Only a little scree in drifts at the wall feet. The cave-in heap (flag) keeps its boulders by design.
- **Rock edge against the black:** no outer ink. The rim is defined from a heavily blurred solid field (`solidField`/`rimValue`), so its outer edge is round and wobbling even where the cliff extrusions of a diagonal tunnel make a staircase, and the rock first darkens toward black and then its alpha fades to 0 exactly at that edge (`rock.feather`): rock sinks into black with no step, no line and no pale ring.
- **Shrine:** stands in a sculpted outcrop of the same blocks (`drawOutcrop`, teal shadows / gold-ochre lit planes) with a carved arch (reveal lit by the fire, one chiselled groove) instead of a frame of stones; no pebble mound anywhere.
- **Ground:** one painted language for all grounds: a softly stepped, warped tone field (brushed patches + slow swell), dirt gathering darker toward every wall foot (`distToWall`), a blurred wear band polished along tunnels and rails, soft scuff dabs, sparse grit, pebbles only in drifts. No crack lines, no blotches, no strata bands; paving only in the overseer's post (worn soft slabs, grime joints); crystal = pale mineral dust, damp = soft wet sheens, hollow = violet stain soaking from the heart. Office ground warmed to gray-brown.
- **Light:** the mine's light rig ambient is `#7c8494` (shared default unchanged); after the multiply, the same lightmap is drawn again with `soft-light` at 0.6 (`LIGHT_GLOW`): pools glow brighter and take the light's colour, the dark between sinks. Sensed gray = 9 + 0.38·L. Measured floor luminance (full preset, control 0): Active 49–98 per zone, Sensed 24–33 — the dimmest Active zone (low gallery 49, sump 52) is ≥ 1.45× the brightest Sensed zone (bunks 33). The overseer's post gets two warm wall torches and a larger, stronger steel-blue lamp (warm/cool separation).
- **Props:** 20 standing props that had none got a contact shadow; props take the local light colour through the coloured lightmap + soft-light pass.
- **Fog:** mine fog warp 20 → 34 cells (fronts between zones bend instead of running straight).
- **Perf:** `drawRailJob` (rails sliced per gravel run / sleeper run / rail), `rimGridJob`, Drawable.job (additive; the shrine outcrop bakes sliced), finer rock/floor yields, mip strips flushed per strip (first level 50k px). Worst bake slice 37 ms (the albedo canvas allocation; no slice > 40 ms, was 75). Frame costs unchanged within noise (idle fit 9.0 → 9.0 ms, zoom 16.1 → 16.0, idle z2 13.2 → 13.5, fade mid 9.3 → 9.7 med; the soft-light pass costs ≈ 0.3 ms).

#### Round 5A (P2 mine render — art in the vector bake: rock, floors, palette; follows ART-REFERENCE.md)
- **Rock is quarried slabs and scree, no Voronoi anywhere.** North-facing cliff faces (wrapping down the west/east sides as the lifted floor foreshortens) are stamped from a library of slab sprites (`paintSlab`, `art/boulders.ts`): angular blocks with chamfered top facets and softened corners, a near-flat front face, a lighter top plane, a darker side facet with sparse hatching, a crack or two, a dark foot, firm ink heavier at the top silhouette and foot; no canvas filters (cheap). Per face column the planner picks one tall slab, a block on a block (upper set back) or a low block in front of a tall one; tops stand 12–26 proud or fall short so the wall's top silhouette is irregular; a short face rises to ≈ 56 units where the rock above has room. Every stamp sits on a dark halo, so slabs are separated by black gaps. Rounded stones (`paintBoulder`, grayer tone) appear only as scree at wall feet, in heaps and in the cave-in. South floor edges get no wall: ink → black (outer) or ink → the island's dark-teal margin.
- **Rock tops:** islands keep a narrow dark-teal margin along a floor's south edge fading (f²) to black; behind a cliff's top the margin is dim and short (0.4·f³ over 0.45·band) so slab tops never sit on a halo; the coverage (albedo alpha) behind faces reaches a full band so 5B's edge sink does not eat the slab tops. Outer rock beyond south/side edges is not drawn (alpha 0 from ≈ 9 units).
- **Palette:** `PAL.mineRock` bluer and darker (no mint); floors (`GROUND`) dark teal-gray per zone (office cool steel, shrine warm), the wear band darker and fainter, the rail bed darker. `lightCast` (bake, `source-atop`): rock and ground inside warm light lean ochre-brown (so teal × gold light reads gold-ochre, not olive), inside the Tally lamp steel-blue.
- **Shrine:** a deep niche cut in a cliff of ochre-leaning slabs (`drawOutcropJob`), arch reveal / pillars / steps (`SHRINE_STONE`) darkened; the eye light 0.7 → 0.45. The remaining glare is additive light (5B request #7).
- **Overseer's post:** the east torch is gone; the post is lit by the steel-blue Tally lamp, one warm torch far down the west wall.
- **Cave-in (`drawCaveInHeap`):** a dark heap mass, six tumbled broken blocks (slabs at odd angles), boulders and stones packed smaller toward the crest with black gaps, three splintered beams, grit spilled in front, a dust fan.

#### Round 5B (P2 mine render — engine: light confinement, sink, stain, seams)
- Light never leaves the rock: static light is baked into a pre-lit layer through a light mask (coverage × ramp
  inside the outline); every dynamic glow is radius-clamped (`safeGlow`); emissives, dust, glints, crest glows and
  ground marks are gated by `keepAt`/`sinkAt`.
- Sink to black: every layer darkens toward `#000` over SINK_D = 84 world units from the void, convex (k^2), so no
  lit lip can sit on the edge. The fog's `seenSink` (FogSource, additive) does the same at Sensed/Active → Void
  fronts (membership-normalised, so deep rock between seen zones never sinks). Void zones measure 100.00 % `#000`.
- Warm light is pushed toward ochre (`lightColor`) and the soft-light pass uses a 45 %-desaturated lightmap: teal ×
  fire never reads lime. Shrine light capped (SHRINE_K 0.55).
- The stain (§14.2): per-zone order field = noisy-cost geodesic distance from the seeds (mouths shared with
  `holdFrom` zones, `holdAt`, else the way to the god) ranked by the zone's ground; the fog thresholds it by the
  creeping Hold (2 s ease-out) inside the Active channel, so the colour front uses the same soft organic falloff as
  Active↔Sensed; a violet streak shimmer runs in a band just ahead of it. View fields `hold/holdFrom/holdAt`.
- Seams: `addLayer('ground'|'props'|'above', fn)` in the colour pass before the composite, with `visibilityAt` and
  `lightAt`, for AMBIENT / VFX / people.
- Rim test split: a boundary counts as a void boundary when its black run reaches true void; black island tops
  entering lit wall faces are art (ART-REFERENCE §2) and are reported separately as `raw`.


#### Round 5B successor #3 (mine engine: fog veil, kept cliff faces, layer cover, day/weather seams)
- No drifting haze over Sensed ground in the mine (`hazeGain 0`): the additive film was the pale veil round the seen area. Sensed is told by gray alone. The stain front's shimmer is thin violet ridges only (no film across the band); this also applies to the town's stain front (shared `fog.ts`).
- The gray (Sensed) layer has no sink of its own: it darkens at the rock's edge exactly like the colour layer (masked lightmap + the shared edge sink). The old quarter-res blurred sink made every Sensed edge a smudge.
- Fog rule, now tested (`__an.fogVeil`, `mine-edges.cjs --fringe`): the fog may only choose between the colour and the gray art and darken it toward black. It never adds light (lift ≤ 0.5 %) and never darkens in a band (≤ 3 %).
- The edge sink no longer eats cliff faces. Wall-face cells, and the slabs rising up to 30 units over them, are kept out of the sink and out of the light-mask fade. Near the void a kept face still sinks over 44 units (convex), so no lighter cap sits on the black. Rim test: a "lip" (brightest point hugging the contour and brighter than what follows) is the defect. A lit slab plane against black is a form and is allowed (ART-REFERENCE §1).
- People fade with the edge sink at their feet (√sink).
- Seam layers are clipped to the world rect and covered by a void cover (opaque over the void and the first 8 units of rock), so a layer can never paint Void or off-art pixels.
- The day clock drives the sky's light down the shaft as a pure function (`shaftSky(t, rain)`). Weather parameters come in through `setAtmos` (wet/water/drip/rain).
#### Round 5B successor #4 (mine engine: ground seam, the wickfolk's things, night, weather, perf)
- **Ground seam** (`src/render/mine/ground.ts`): the engine takes the rock + floor albedo from an outside painter
  (`MineGround.paint(g, rect)`: light-free form shading and ink, alpha 0 in the void; optional `wallFace` mask on the
  4-unit grid) and keeps everything else: props, rails, light mask, lightmap, edge sink, Sensed gray, stain, fog.
  Default stays the engine's own walls until the terrain's reworked rock is adopted (`setMineGround(terrainGround)`,
  `?ground=terrain`). The sliced bake waits on the worker bake with `yield 'wait'` (ends the slice).
- **Things** (`src/render/mine/things.ts`, theme §7): every human-world prop in the mine replaced — stalk-timber
  frames and posts (pale, fibrous, nodes, cord lashing), shard-lamps (crystal splinter in a horn cage, cold white) on
  horn pegs in the wall and on stalk tripods, ember-pots on horn brackets in place of torches, gourds / baskets / hide
  bundles in place of barrels / crates / sacks / buckets, horn picks and shell scoops, shell carts on stalk chassis,
  the Council's post as a cast block with panels and the square-in-square mark, knot-cords on stalk rails, a
  notch-tally board (V-notches, deep every fifth: no letters, no slashed human tallies), a horn balance with shell
  pans, sealed cast canisters, a lamp-staff rack, a stalk boom; carved gold nooks of the Flame on every level; pit-beast
  stalls by the rails; the lift as a cast frame with sheave and a crystal-fired engine (violet port). Skulls → shed
  shell plates. Shard-lamp light is cold white (`SHARD_LIGHT`, not pushed warm); ember-pots stay the warm work light.
- **Night** (the view's `dayT`, DECISIONS §92): a second light state = the lightmap with only what is left burning (`NIGHT_BURN`:
  ember-pots 10 %, shard-lamps 70 %, Council lamp 85 %, the Flame's candles, crystals, the god, moss and water 100 %)
  over an ambient 40 % darker, stored as a ratio to the day lightmap (LM_RES) and multiplied over the lit layer by the
  night amount (one small multiply per frame; built lazily, 4 ms a frame, the first time night falls). Flames, halos
  and flicker are gated by the same burn share. Noon is the day look; a view without an hour is at night.
- **Weather** (`setAtmos`, `src/render/mine/weather.ts`): wet = runnels down the wet faces and sheen patches on the
  floor, coloured by the light falling there, inside the light mask and the edge sink, per zone; water = the sump pools
  grow and the low gallery / sump chambers flood (clipped to the floor); drip = drops falling down damp faces with a
  ring at the foot (rate ×); rain = streaks falling down the shaft and mist at its foot.
- **Perf**: lit tiles 256 → 128, yields between every pass of the light-mask job and between the fog's near/far blurs.

### P2T — Sculpted terrain
- **Method (owner's choice):** rock and ground are a *height field* shaded per pixel with the real lights, then inked and hatched — no outlined boulders anywhere. Module `src/render/terrain/` (generic: input = floor polygons + per-polygon material + pools + routes + lights; the mine adapter is `terrain/mine.ts`, read-only on `mine/layout|geometry|dressing`, `art/palette`).
- **Fields** at 2 world units per cell: floor mask (polygon union, smoothed, edge wobbled ±5 units so the wall foot wanders), signed distance to the floor edge, nearest-floor material (EDT with feature index), route distance, pools. Materials and ground styles live on a 4-unit grid blurred over ≈ 24 units so zones blend softly.
- **3/4 cue:** an exact anisotropic one-sided distance (`wallParam`) — rock north of a floor reaches full cliff height over 92 units (the visible face), sideways over 22, south over 12. The parameter is blurred so floor-edge wiggles do not extrude as grooves. Every rock mass (islands too) ends in the void after a rim band (north 132 / side 44 / south 36 units), like the reference's black between tunnels; `TerrainSpec.solidIslands` keeps islands as solid rock tops instead (for the town).
- **Rock form:** cliff height 128 above the floor. Faces are Voronoi blocks (cells stretched ×1.18 / ×0.8, 56 units), each a tilted plane with a *linear* chamfer toward every neighbour (exact bisector distance → flat chamfers meeting in crisp ridges), a level top band under each block's northern edge (3/4 block tops), and about half of them split again by a 27-unit Voronoi with shallower chamfers. Past the lip the top breaks into 80-unit slabs. Scree: small chamfered chips (inked) along the wall foot and sparse stones on floors. No round bumps.
- **Light:** each mine light becomes a point light 60 units higher than its mine height (so faces and tops catch it like a painter's light), radius ×0.8 (crystals ×0.56, god glow ×0.44: tight violet glows), falloff (1 − d/r)^1.35, colour 28 % toward white, the mine's per-zone gains (sump 1.45, low gallery 1.35, east 1.2, lift 1.1). A directional lightmap on a 2-unit grid stores irradiance, dominant direction and coherence; cast shadows are a soft ray-march through the height field. Ambient (`PAL.ambient`) is weighted toward surfaces facing the 3/4 viewer plus a cool key from the upper left.
- **Painterly, not rendered:** rock value is *per plane* — form value from orientation (tops lightest, key-facing sides light, fronts mid, away sides dark) quantised into 4 steps (steps fall on facet edges because normals are constant per plane); the real light's Lambert is quantised in 2 soft steps; final luminance bands with noise wobble at 30 %. Surface colour = albedo × light hue (62 %) + a share of the light's own colour (painted highlight: teal rock turns gold, not olive). Shadows lean violet; a faint pale edge on key-facing chamfers; low-frequency hue/value variation per block and brush streaks. Exposure 1.18 with a soft shoulder.
- **Ink and hatching:** dark ink (#090a0d) at the wall foot (weight wanders 0.6–1.7 px), the lip, the void silhouette (heaviest), block creases (weight by step height, heavier on the side turned from the key light, swelling and tapering along the line, broken in strong light), thin lines on secondary splits and chips, hairline floor cracks in patches. Hatching: one diagonal stroke direction per block, broken strokes, only on faces turned from the key and the lights.
- **Floors:** tone + texture + wear in the same language: soft patches, sandy grain, worn lighter route centres with darker ruts (packed earth), grime at the wall foot; packed ochre (haul), cold teal-gray (crystal), dark damp stone with wet patches and sheen (damp), gray-brown (work), soot-warm stained earth (shrine), violet-gray (hollow), red-brown (quarters), warped running-bond slate flagstones (office). Pools: shore-to-depth teal gradient, ripples, reflections of the lights' colour, a lit shore line, inked edge.
- **Output (`bakeTerrain(spec, opts)` → `TerrainBake`):** `lit` (recommended: fully lit, alpha 0 in the void), `form` (light-free albedo × form + ink, for a renderer that keeps its own multiply lightmap), `data` (1 px / 2 units: normal xy, height, AO — for dynamic lights such as the god's pulse). Flicker on the lit layer: `lightFlickerDelta` draws the ±k difference per light. Bake: Web Workers (up to 4, each shades a band of rows after redoing the cheap fields/heights/lightmap), main thread only uploads rows in ≈ 1 MB strips; fallback: main-thread time slices. Deterministic (integer hashes, no `Math.random`), runs in Node.
- **Dependencies:** none added.

#### Round 5B successor #5 (mine engine: terrain adopted, warning gone — scope per §19)
- **The terrain is the mine's default ground** (`src/render/mine/ground.ts`: default provider `terrainMineGround`, which also
  publishes the `wallFace` / `floor` masks). Every consumer of `MineRenderer` gets it with no extra call; the engine's own slab
  walls stay available via `setMineGround(null)` (`?ground=engine` on dev/mine.html). Terrain paints rock, floor, shrine niche
  and pools; the engine keeps rails, props, timber, light, sink, Sensed gray, stain, fog.
- **Edge sink over an installed ground** (lit.ts): reach `GROUND_SINK_D` = 60 units (engine walls keep `SINK_D` = 84), kept
  cliff faces ramp over `KEEP_IN` = 18 (was 44). The terrain already darkens its rock tops to black before the void, so the
  long engine ramp only ate the tops of its north cliffs and smudged island edges.
- **Pinholes:** enclosed void specks (≤ 120 mask cells) inside the ground's rock count as rock for the light mask and
  sink (each grew an ~80-unit black blot).
- **Boot:** no synchronous path may wait on a worker (`runSync` throws on `'wait'`; a sync build uses the engine walls).
  The whole-world lit bake is CPU arithmetic (`cpuBakeJob`, same passes as the canvas tile path, 53 s → ~4 s under load)
  and progressive: ¼-res at boot, full resolution refined between frames and committed at once.
- **No readback warning:** the two readbacks from canvases that already had a non-`willReadFrequently` context
  (`cellLuminanceJob`, `nightRatioJob`) now read a private `willReadFrequently` copy.

#### Round 4 (P2T successor 5 — cliff faces, the engine seam)
